rule: the reading whose structural/variables ratio on the files of the 32-39 bit band
  is closest to 0.92 (MCSS Table 3); on a tie within 0.05, the one whose mean
  number of variables is closest to 11.9
  (tie: |ratio - 0.92| at most 0.05 above the smallest one; band and eligible
  files as in the selection, band recomputed on each reading's mcss_mod table; ratio of
  the means)

structural / variables                      1.37                0.71                0.63      0.92
|ratio - 0.92|                              0.45                0.21                0.29
|n_vars - 11.9|                             1.58                0.98                1.47

outcome: names.single_letter = extend_except_loop for mcss (closest ratio)
